# Skills · C · part 45

988 entries, changelog to changelog-writer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **changelog**: Use when adding a changelog entry, setting up CHANGES.md / CHANGELOG.md, documenting user-facing changes in a release, or writing a migration note for a breaking change. Triggers on "add to changelog", "CHANGES.md entry", "document this release", "release notes", "breaking change migration note", "what's new". Do NOT use for the project README (that's writing-readmes) or per-file license headers (that's reuse-headers).
